Deliberately **not** written as `Assert.Equal(NotRequired, …)`, which would be a red test for unscheduled work, and **the work was shaped so that when the data slice lands EXACTLY ONE TEST FAILS** — which is why `BillOfSupplyPosAndPostingGuardTests` was re-based onto de-oiled cake too, so a Desktop failure would not read as an `IsBillOfSupply`/BIL-routing regression. **THAT DATA SLICE IS UNSCHEDULED IN plan.md.** · **`PINNED_the_return_and_challan_branches_cannot_be_posted_so_they_are_unreachable_today`** — fails the day the challan path is wired up, by design. · `RULING_a_composition_movement_carrying_posted_forward_tax_still_files_BIL` **silently depends on `GstConfig.EWayIntraStateApplicable` to reach `PrepareRecord` at all — measured: clearing that flag reds `PINNED_GAP_…` ×3 and that RULING** (recorded in the fixture's comment rather than re-based unasked). · **CTRL+B IS FREE AND RESERVED** and must reach nothing until Basis of Values is built; its safety depends on `CanQuickJump`'s `e.KeyModifiers == KeyModifiers.None` guard, **which must not be loosened.** · **Diverged-rule D6's `ApplyRounding` divergence on negatives is PINNED AS CORRECT** — but **on the INTEREST side only; no test references `PayHeadRoundingMethod.Upward`/`.Downward`, so a "cleanup" converting the PAYROLL side to magnitude semantics would still pass green.** · **`OneRuleDriftLockTests` is a textual ratchet, not a proof of uniqueness**, and says so in the file. · **`EWayBillService.DocTypeOf` returning "INV" for every Sales voucher was pinned `PINNED_UNVERIFIED_…` rather than laundered** (superseded by W0-8/W0-9, but the naming convention is now precedent) — **deliberately left alone at the time because "stamping a government-portal field from memory is exactly what R7 forbids": NIC's official master-codes list refused automated retrieval and the unofficial secondaries disagreed on whether `BIL` means "Bill" or "Bill of Supply".**

- **⚠️ LESSONS FROM THIS SESSION — these cost real time; reinforce them:**
  1. **A GREEN SUITE HIDES BUGS, AND THIS RANGE IS THE PROOF: review found a real defect on essentially every slice, several of which had passed a full green suite.** Vacuous assertions found in this range alone: `Assert.Equal(partyDebit, postedPartyLeg)` comparing a value with itself · `Assert.Equal("(none)", absent ?? "(none)")`, which is `x == (null ?? x)` and called no consumer at all · `The_desktop_wrappers_never_answer_differently_from_the_engine` with 2 of 3 comparisons constant on every iteration · `Rows.Count > 0` protecting 12 of 44 cases · `IsEmptyState` matching only `"No "`-prefixed prose · the e-Way "exhaustive conformance" test with 4 of 6 branches unreachable and 3 of 5 columns transcribed · and a test that touches no product code at all (`BasisPointFormatsAreIdenticalForEveryRepresentableRate`). **⇒ ASSERT NON-VACUITY EXPLICITLY — require each predicate to take BOTH values somewhere in the matrix, and run lock patterns over faithful reconstructions of the copies you removed. "The scan found nothing" must not be able to mean "the pattern matches nothing".**
  2. **A SUITE WRITTEN AGAINST THE IMPLEMENTATION CANNOT DETECT THAT THE IMPLEMENTATION NEVER MET THE SPECIFICATION.** Stated on `4223996`, re-stated on `23e0df1`, and both times the same fix worked. **⇒ TRANSCRIBE THE PRIMARY SOURCE INTO A CONFORMANCE TABLE AND ASSERT AGAINST IT** — `EWayPartAOrientationTests` (28 rows × 5 columns) and `EInvoiceInv01SchemaConformanceTests` (the NIC workbook) are the shape to copy.
  3. **THE ROOT CAUSE OF THE INVENT-RATHER-THAN-CLONE PATTERN WAS PHYSICAL, NOT CULTURAL: the git-ignored `tally/` PDFs lived only in the main checkout, so every agent dispatched into a worktree was told to clone TallyPrime and was PHYSICALLY UNABLE TO OPEN IT.** Corroborated by the kickoff's own measurement (`docs/NEXT_SESSION_KICKOFF.md:50`) — **only 13 of 489 source files cite the corpus while 331 cite Indian statute, because statute was reachable on the web and the corpus was not.** **🔴 THIS LINE PREVIOUSLY SAID "the census corroborates" AND THE CENSUS DOES NOT CONTAIN THE FIGURE: `grep -nE "\b489\b|\b331\b" docs/full-clone-census.md` returns ZERO hits. That is exactly the failure class Lesson 10 and the "34 false claims" bullet above are about, committed inside the entry that names it. ⚠️ The kickoff line ITSELF says "corroborated by the census", so the mis-attribution ORIGINATES THERE — it is item (d) on the kickoff bullet above, which is why that bullet now reads FOUR places, not three.** **⇒ CREATE THE CORPUS JUNCTION IN EVERY NEW WORKTREE FIRST: `New-Item -ItemType Junction -Path "<worktree>\tally" -Target "…\Apex Solutions(end)\tally"`. `.gitignore:73` keeps it invisible to git, so R4 holds.**
  4. **RUN REVIEW LENSES SEQUENTIALLY, AND PASS THE COMPLETE FINDING LIST TO THE FIX AGENT — NEVER A SUMMARY.** Parallel lenses die together and lose everything; sequential ones journal as they complete, which directly saved 8 findings. Summarising left **four** findings unaddressed on W0-9, **two of them vacuous-test defects**, carried openly on `50fa892` and closed only in `439220d`. **⇒ SEQUENTIAL LENSES, VERBATIM HAND-OFF.**
  5. **AGENTS DIE CONSTANTLY (13 times this session per the kickoff) AND ALWAYS LEAVE FILES ON DISK; WORKTREE DIRECTORIES ALSO VANISH OUTRIGHT.** Two commits in this range (`b12b8cb`, `f2abdbb`) are the recovered output of agents that died after writing to disk and never reported, and `stream-b`'s worktree directory was deleted — its work survived only because it had been committed. **⇒ CHECK THE TREE AFTER EVERY DEATH, AND COMMIT TO PRESERVE — but LABEL an unreviewed commit UNREVIEWED IN ITS OWN BODY, as `b12b8cb` does. ⚠️ AND COMMITTING IS NOT ENOUGH WHEN NOTHING IS ON A REMOTE — see WHAT REMAINS item 1.**
  6. **A TRANSPORT-LAYER REFUSAL IS NOT A MISSING PAGE, AND READING IT AS ONE SHIPPED A DEFECT.** `WebFetch` gets **HTTP 403** from `docs.ewaybillgst.gov.in` and a **TLS chain error** from `taxinformation.cbic.gov.in`; the Central Tax (Rate) PDF on that host is malformed (`Couldn't read xref table`) and its Integrated Tax (Rate) twin carries the same Schedule; `curl` fetched `EInvoice_Schema.xlsx` directly at HTTP 200. **⇒ WHEN A SOURCE REFUSES, TRY ANOTHER TRANSPORT BEFORE CONCLUDING IT IS UNOBTAINABLE — AND ALWAYS STATE WHICH METHOD YOU USED.**
  7. **ODD-PAISA FIXTURES ALWAYS.** A ±₹0.50 defect survived this project's whole life under six round-number assertions; `f2abdbb` moved its fixture to **₹25,417.83** for exactly this reason, and `a12e651` used odd quantities so a stock imbalance could not be a rounding artefact. **⇒ NEVER ASSERT MONEY OR QUANTITY ON A ROUND NUMBER.**
  8. **`isolation: 'worktree'` CUTS FROM `main`, NOT FROM THE CURRENT BRANCH.** **⇒ VERIFY `Schema.CurrentVersion` IN EVERY NEW WORKTREE BEFORE BUILDING** — check the parents, not the branch name. (Compounds the 2026-08-03 lesson: **liveness lies, and so does the working tree.**)
  9. **WHEN A "DUPLICATION DEFECT" IS INVESTIGATED PROPERLY, SOME OF IT IS NOT ONE — AND REFUSING A ROW IS A RESULT.** Diverged-rule D8 was a false positive, D6's divergence is two correct statutory answers, the `Apportion` zero-guard was unreachable, and the caller-side guards it looked redundant beside are load-bearing. **⇒ PROVE THE SYMPTOM BEFORE UNIFYING; a false unification silently changes one domain's arithmetic to suit another's, and a shared home for a rule that does not vary is churn justified by a defect that is not real.**
  10. **A FIGURE EVERYONE REPEATS IS NOT A MEASURED FIGURE.** "34 false claims" and "nine internal contradictions" are quoted across this project as census findings and **are in no census section**; the pointer resolves to a 15-row table. **⇒ RE-DERIVE A NUMBER BEFORE CITING IT, EVEN WHEN THE DOCUMENT CITING IT IS OUR OWN — this is the exact failure class the census was written to remove.**

  7. **THE PRINTED-VS-POSTED MONEY DEFECT IS ONLY HALF CLOSED.** §206C TCS rides the party leg and has no `InvoicePrintData` member, so the "printed total == posted party leg" invariant is stated for **NON-TCS** sales only (`plan.md:1719`), and **the item-path footing guard must be sequenced AFTER a TCS row lands or it would refuse every TCS invoice** (`plan.md:1722-1729`). **A supply taxable at 0% posts no leg for either pass to read** — a statutory question needing a rate snapshotted onto the posted line, i.e. **a schema change, not a print-path patch.**
  8. **THE `GstReturnJson` INVENTED KEYS** — the third instance of the statutory-payload class, dead code today, and already plan item **W0-4** (`plan.md:1554`), which is GATED on A14/R7 confirming the GSTN key schema — and the **`CostAllocationStrictness` naming debt** (~13 files across four test projects). Both are known, scoped and safe to defer — **but the first stops being safe the moment a production caller appears.**
  9. **ZERO-RATED / SEZ HAS NO CONCEPT IN `GstTaxability`** — an exempt supply to an overseas place of supply prints a bill of supply while e-invoicing as an **export**; pinned by a characterization test rather than silently normalised. **And an overseas place of supply cannot be recorded through a validated master edit at all**: `IndianState.All` carries 97 but neither 96 nor 99, and `PartyGstDetails.EnsureValid` rejects anything outside that list — **⚠️ adding them is NOT a safe local edit, because `Gstin.Validate` checks a GSTIN's leading two digits against the same list and would start accepting GSTINs beginning "96"/"99", which do not exist. Splitting the place-of-supply domain from the GSTIN-prefix domain is its own slice.**
  10. **PERSISTED PRE-FIX e-WAY RECORDS STILL CANNOT BE FULLY CORRECTED.** `Rehydrate` narrows it, but a legacy row cannot recover `BIL` for a composition/exempt sale nor `3 Export` for an overseas place of supply — neither is recoverable from the stored triple — **and there is still NO in-app path to re-prepare: the three fields are get-only, `PrepareRecord` refuses a second record for the same voucher, and `Cancel` requires a Generated status while these rows sit in the ordinary offline resting state, Pending.**
  11. **THE USER-BLOCKED MEASUREMENTS — `docs/tallyprime-valuation-test-books.md` T1–T13.** T3 decides the Average Cost rewrite; T1/T2/T4 settle negative-stock and per-godown valuation; T8 unfreezes the `CalendarMonth` divisor left deliberately wrong at `InterestCalculation.cs:481`; T8–T11 unblock four fixes that cannot be written without a measured answer. **⚠️ T7 needs a third option added before it is run.** **Nothing here proceeds on a guess — this project has already reverted eight attempts that did.**

## Phase 10.12 Wave 0 — W0-12: A SUB-PAISA FIGURE POISONED THE IN-MEMORY COMPANY, AND TWELVE SAVES NEVER ROLLED BACK (branch `claude/apex-wrong-figures-bc45f4`, commit `fa651ae` on `9dfb317`, 2026-08-14) — **schema v50 UNCHANGED · BUILT, REVIEWED (3 sequential lenses, 14 findings, NONE rejected) and 🔴 PUSHED — the first commit of this campaign to reach a remote · NO PR (deferred by the user) · `origin/main` still `c655dc2`**

- **▶ WHAT THIS SLICE IS.** One commit, `fa651ae`, 4 files, **+1,576 / −41** (`plan.md` 184 · `src/Apex.Desktop/ViewModels/GstConfigViewModel.cs` 330 · two NEW test files, `tests/Apex.Desktop.Tests/StatutoryConfigSubPaisaGuardTests.cs` 762 and `StatutoryConfigSiblingRollbackTests.cs` 341). It is `plan.md` item **W0-12** (**`plan.md:2068-2256`** — re-derived 2026-08-14 against the working tree; this read `:2046-2205` in a 2,502-line file, and BOTH numbers moved when the R5/R6 corrections and the W0-2 gate landed, taking `plan.md` to **2,553** lines), which **discharges W0-11's carry-forward (a)** — the highest-value open item that slice created. **Schema-clean:** `Schema.CurrentVersion` is **50** (`src/Apex.Persistence.Sqlite/Schema.cs:146`) and stays 50; the three columns have existed since v37 and their stored shape does not change. **The commit body is long and it is accurate — read it (`git log -1 --format=%B fa651ae`) rather than a summary; every file:line in it was re-opened and re-verified against the working tree while this entry was written, and the two corrections that came out of that are named below.**
- **🔴 THE DEFECT, AND ITS BLAST RADIUS STATED ACCURATELY — IT IS "THIS SESSION", NOT "THIS BOOK", AND THE DIFFERENCE IS THE WHOLE POINT.** `6a0268a` (the "one rule, one home" slice) replaced the three `SqliteCompanyStore` establishment money writes — the **gratuity cap** (`:4621`), the **bonus calculation ceiling** (`:4628`) and the **bonus minimum wage** (`:4629`) — with the **throwing** `Paisa.FromDecimal`, and did **not** give their only writer the paired front-line guard the tax-declaration screen already had, because `GstConfigViewModel.cs` **is not among that commit's 46 files**. `TryParseWholeRupees` was a plain `decimal.TryParse` **despite its name**, and the only further check was `< 0m`. So the throw replaced a silent truncation with something strictly worse: `PayrollService.EnableGratuity` sets `PayrollStatutoryEnabled` and then `GratuityConfig` on the **shared `Company` aggregate BEFORE `_storage.Save`**, neither config validates paisa-exactness, so a sub-paisa cap **entered the aggregate**, the store threw, the catch reported the message and returned false — **and left the bad config in memory**. Every LATER save of that Company then threw, including from the **99 `_storage.Save(` call sites in `src/Apex.Desktop/` (re-measured at `fa651ae`: 99, of which 18 are in `GstConfigViewModel.cs`)** that mostly have no `try`/`catch`, so an unrelated screen crashed long after the F11 keystroke that caused it. `EnableStatutoryBonus` is the identical shape. **🔴 BUT THE DATABASE WAS NEVER CORRUPTED: `SqliteCompanyStore.Save` opens `using var tx = _connection.BeginTransaction();` (`src/Apex.Persistence.Sqlite/SqliteCompanyStore.cs:1747`) and commits at the end, so a throw inside `InsertCompany` rolls the whole write back.** "Every later save throws until restart" is TRUE; "the book is corrupted" is FALSE. **W0-11's carry-forward (a) did not draw that line and this slice does — do not let the entry drift back into overstating it.**
- **🔴 THE CATCH DID NOT EVEN REVERT THE TOGGLE — THE REVERT WAS NOT WEAK HERE, IT WAS *INVERTED* BY THE VERY MUTATION IT EXISTED TO UNDO.** `RevertGratuityToggle` implements the doc comment's promise "the toggle reverts to the real company state" as `GratuityEnabled = (_company.GratuityConfig is not null)` — **which the failed call had just made `true`**. The toggle stayed ON over a poisoned config, and `AcceptStatutoryConfig` (`GstConfigViewModel.cs:1788`) re-runs `ApplyGratuity()` on every accept while the toggle is on, **re-poisoning from the keyboard too**. `RevertBonusToggle` is identical. **⇒ RESTORING THE AGGREGATE FIRST IS WHAT MAKES THOSE HELPERS START WORKING AT ALL — the restore must land BEFORE THE TOGGLE IS RE-SYNCED in every one of the twelve methods, and the source carries that as a comment at each site.** **⚠️ PRECISION, BECAUSE THIS SENTENCE USED TO READ "before `Revert*Toggle()` in every one of the twelve" AND ONLY HALF OF THEM HAVE SUCH A HELPER:** the **six `Apply*`** methods do precede a literal `Revert*Toggle()` call (`ApplyPf` `:858`/`:882`, `ApplyEsi` `:929`/`:950`, `ApplyPt` `:1005`/`:1049`, `ApplySalaryTds` `:1151`, `ApplyGratuity` `:1215`/`:1228`/`:1253`, `ApplyBonus` `:1309`…`:1356`); the **six `On*Changed`** handlers have **no helper at all** — they inline the same re-derivation as an open comparison (e.g. `OnMaintainBatchwiseDetailsChanged` `:663-664`, `if (MaintainBatchwiseDetails != _company.MaintainBatchwiseDetails)`), which is the identical trap in plainer clothing and needs the restore first for exactly the same reason. This is the transferable shape: **a revert that re-derives its target from the state it is meant to revert is a guaranteed no-op, and it reads as working code.**
- **▶ WHAT SHIPPED — line numbers post-change, in `src/Apex.Desktop/ViewModels/GstConfigViewModel.cs` unless stated.** **(a) THE FRONT-LINE GUARD** — `TryStatutoryRupees` (`:1407`), **three call sites** (`:1223` gratuity cap, `:1323` calculation ceiling, `:1331` minimum wage), **four ordered branches, opened and read in the shipped source:** parse/sign (`:1411`, reusing each field's pre-existing message **byte-identically**, so the non-numeric and negative wording is unchanged) → **magnitude ceiling** `MaxStatutoryRupees` (`:1384`, `92_233_720_368_547_758m` = `long.MaxValue` paisa floored to the rupee) at `:1423` → **paisa-exactness** `new Money(value).IsPaisaExact` at `:1430` → **whole rupee** `value % 1m != 0m` at `:1444`. **The ordering is load-bearing, not cosmetic:** the magnitude branch must sit ABOVE the two below it because those branches THEMSELVES throw on a big enough decimal (the paisa predicate scales by 100 and overflows past `decimal.MaxValue ÷ 100`; the store's conversion narrows to `long`), and sub-paisa must sit above whole-rupee so ₹19,99,999.995 gets the specific "finer than a paisa" wording rather than the generic one. **(b) CAPTURE-AND-RESTORE OF THE AGGREGATE, IN TWELVE METHODS** (see the count correction below): `OnMaintainBatchwiseDetailsChanged` (`:647`), `OnSetComponentsBomChanged` (`:676`), `OnEnableMultiplePriceLevelsChanged` (`:702`), `OnPayrollEnabledChanged` (`:754`, which must capture **both** payroll flags because `DisablePayroll` clears them both), `OnPayrollStatutoryEnabledChanged` (`:798`), `ApplyPf` (`:847`), `ApplyEsi` (`:919`), `ApplyPt` (`:995`), `ApplySalaryTds` (`:1129`), `ApplyGratuity` (`:1201`), `ApplyBonus` (`:1298`), `OnDefineBomComponentTypeChanged` (`:1486`). **`ApplyPt` captures two extra FIELDS rather than just the reference** — `SetProfessionalTaxState` and the line beside it edit `StateCode` and `RegistrationNumber` **in place** on the existing config, and `PtConfig.ResolveSlab` selects the deduction slab table **BY `StateCode`**, so a failed save otherwise left the session computing Professional Tax off a state the book does not have: **a silent wrong-FIGURES divergence, which is a different severity from a loud crash.** **(c) THE CATCH FILTER STOPPED DECIDING TWO THINGS AT ONCE** — `TrySave` (`:1824`) takes an optional `restore` which runs **unconditionally in every catch**, and `IsReportableSaveFailure` (`:1855`) separately decides report-or-rethrow over `InvalidOperationException`, `ArgumentException`, `OverflowException`, `IOException`, `UnauthorizedAccessException`, `DbException`. The old inline `when (ex is InvalidOperationException or ArgumentException)` decided both at once, so **the rollback was unreachable for the most ordinary failure a desktop accounting app has** — SQLITE_BUSY from a second instance holding the write lock, READONLY, FULL, and `IOException`/`UnauthorizedAccessException` opening the company file (`SqliteCompanyStore` has **zero** catch blocks of its own, so these propagate raw). **Five DISABLE branches** — Gratuity, Bonus, PF, ESI, PT — now restore the cleared enrolment through that `restore` parameter (`configBeforeClear`). **(d) THE `"0.##"` LOADERS** — `GratuityCapText` (`:1187`), `BonusCalculationCeilingText` (`:1285`) and `BonusMinimumWageText` (`:1286`) render with `ToString("0.##", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)` instead of a truncating `(long)` cast, which is lossless for every amount an INTEGER-paisa store can hold.
- **🔴 THE ONE DELIBERATE BEHAVIOUR CHANGE — A FRACTIONAL RUPEE THE F11 STATUTORY-CONFIGURATION SCREEN ACCEPTED BEFORE IS NOW REFUSED.** A gratuity cap of `7000.50`, or a bonus calculation ceiling / minimum wage of the same shape, gets a message instead of being stored. **It is justified because that input was ALREADY being destroyed — just later, and in silence:** the two loaders re-rendered the stored amount through a truncating `(long)` cast, so a stored ₹7,000.55 came back as `7000`, and the next Ctrl+A accept **re-applied that truncated figure over the establishment's real one with no message at all** — the user was shown one number, the book held another, and the difference vanished. Refusing at the keystroke replaces a silent rewrite with a refusal that can be acted on, and the `"0.##"` loaders mean a legacy or imported fractional figure is now shown IN FULL and refused out loud. **⚠️ THE CONTRACT THIS RESTS ON HAD ONE LEG OVERSTATED AND WAS CORRECTED RATHER THAN LEANED ON:** `GratuityCapText` and `BonusCalculationCeilingText` document whole rupees, **`BonusMinimumWageText` did NOT** (its doc is amended in this commit to say so), the three XAML placeholders are example **VALUES**, not constraints, and the domain's own `BonusConfig.MinimumWage` validates only `< 0m` — **so a fractional minimum wage stays domain-legal; the whole-rupee rule is this SCREEN's, and it is now written where it is enforced.**
- **🔴 THE DECIMAL-COMMA DEFECT — `TryParseWholeRupees` MANUFACTURED A 100× WRONG FIGURE, SILENTLY, AND NOBODY WAS LOOKING FOR IT.** It stripped **every** comma with **no positional check**, so a decimal comma was read as a grouping separator: **`"7000,55"` → `700055`, and the establishment's ₹7,000.55 was stored as ₹7,00,055.00 — accepted with no message.** The fix (`:1462`) requires the group after the LAST comma to be exactly three digits, which is true of both the Indian 2-2-3 and the invariant 3-3-3 rendering, so genuine grouping still parses and a decimal comma is refused. **This was found by REVIEW, not by the design, and it is a third behaviour change belonging to neither half of the commit split that was proposed (below).**
- **▶ THE TESTS — 43 new cases across the two NEW files, and the total reconciles exactly against the gate delta.** Re-counted at `fa651ae`: `StatutoryConfigSubPaisaGuardTests.cs` = **12 `[Fact]` + 18 `[InlineData]` = 30**; `StatutoryConfigSiblingRollbackTests.cs` = **9 `[Fact]` + 4 `[InlineData]` = 13**; **30 + 13 = 43 = the measured Desktop delta.** Fixtures are **ODD sub-paisa throughout** — ₹19,99,999.995 (`:198`, `:229`, `:246`), ₹6,999.995 (`:247`), ₹5,721.005 (`:248`), the ₹7,000.55 loader round-trip (`:588-622`) and the `"7000,55"` decimal comma (`:725`) — because **a round figure is paisa-exact and asserts nothing**, and the valid figures are deliberately never the defaults so a green assertion proves the typed value was carried rather than a default surviving. **⚠️ NO SINGLE TEST IS "THE" RED PROOF and the record must not pretend otherwise:** three defects were fixed together, so each has its own pin. `SubPaisaGratuityCapIsRejectedAndNeverEntersTheCompany` is red against the slice as a whole but goes **GREEN on the rollback alone**, so the pin for the guard BY ITSELF is the test asserting the message names the field and is not the store's raw persistence wording; likewise `Assert.False(page.ApplyGratuity())` passes on HEAD for the wrong reason and is never a red signal on its own.
- **▶ THE GATE (measured by the main loop itself at `fa651ae` — FOUR separate per-project runs, full SDK path `C:\Users\dkpho\.dotnet\dotnet.exe`, no pipes):** build **0 Warning(s) 0 Error(s)** · **Ledger 1555 · Io 389 · Sqlite 215 · Desktop 2056** (2 m 46 s, **Failed 0**). Baseline at `9dfb317`: **Ledger 1555 · Io 389 · Sqlite 215 · Desktop 2013** — **so +43 Desktop and nothing else moved.** **Per `plan.md` §6.2 a gate is the FOUR per-project counts and NEVER the total alone.** Schema **v50** (`src/Apex.Persistence.Sqlite/Schema.cs:146`).
- **⚠️ LESSONS FROM THIS SLICE — this is the reusable part; the rest is history:**
  1. **🔴 THREE REVIEW FINDINGS WERE PROVED BY *MUTATION*, NOT BY ARGUMENT — AND A GREEN SUITE PROVED NOTHING AGAIN, ONE SLICE AFTER THAT EXACT LESSON WAS LAST WRITTEN DOWN (`memory.md` lesson 1 on the `9dfb317` entry, the immediate parent commit).** The reviewer **stripped a line and re-ran the full Desktop suite to show it still passed**, which proved that **both new disable-branch restores were DEAD CODE** and that **`ApplyBonus`'s rollback and the `PayrollStatutoryEnabled` restore were UNPINNED**. Each is now pinned by a test that goes red when its own line is removed **and nothing else does**. **⇒ THE ONLY EVIDENCE A ROLLBACK LINE IS ALIVE IS THAT DELETING IT TURNS EXACTLY ONE TEST RED. Adding a test beside a fix is not verification; neutralising the fix and watching the suite is.**
  2. **🔴 A GUARD THAT LOOKS TOTAL CAN HAVE A HOLE AT THE TOP.** A **17-digit** figure passed **every** branch of the first draft — it parsed, it was non-negative, `IsPaisaExact` returned **TRUE** — and then overflowed `long` inside the store's `ToPaisaExact`, raising an `OverflowException` that **no filter on the screen treated as a domain refusal**, so it escaped `ApplyGratuity`, `AcceptStatutoryConfig` and the Ctrl+A handler entirely **and** skipped the restore lines sitting inside that unmatched catch. **⇒ CHECK MAGNITUDE FIRST, ABOVE EVERY PREDICATE THAT ITSELF ARITHMETICS ON THE VALUE. A refusal has to be a message; a validator that throws is not a validator.**
  3. **🔴 THE REVIEW FOUND FOUR MORE SIBLINGS OF THE SAME DEFECT ON THE SAME SCREEN THAT THE DESIGN HAD NOT NAMED — AND CORRECTLY REFUSED TO HALF-FIX A FIFTH.** The design scoped **two** methods; `AcceptStatutoryConfig` (`:1788`) actually runs `Apply` → `ApplyTds` → `ApplyTcs` → **`ApplyPf` → `ApplyEsi` → `ApplyPt` → `ApplySalaryTds`** → `ApplyGratuity` → `ApplyBonus` and **discards the `bool` each returns**, so one Ctrl+A against a company whose save fails left PF, ESI and PT in memory while the rolled-back `.db` held none of them. All four were fixed here. **The fifth was refused on the merits and recorded as named residue instead:** `OnEnableJobOrderProcessingChanged` (`:729`) routes through `JobWorkService.SetEnabled` (`src/Apex.Ledger/Services/JobWorkService.cs:44-58`), which also stamps `IsActive` / `UseForJobWork` / `AllowConsumption` on **every Job-Work voucher type**, so restoring one bool would not undo the mutation — it needs a per-type capture. **⇒ REFUSING A ROW IS A RESULT (this project's ninth standing lesson, earned again). A half-fix that leaves the aggregate mutated is worse than an untouched defect, because it LOOKS closed.**
  4. **▶ A12 DECLINED THE REQUESTED TWO-WAY COMMIT SPLIT — AND DID IT IN THE COMMIT BODY RATHER THAN SILENTLY.** The body carries a **`WHY THIS IS ONE COMMIT`** section (read it verbatim; it is the precedent). The requested split was (i) guard + rollback + tests, (ii) the whole-rupee rejection + the lossy loaders, and it was attempted on paper first. Its recorded reasons: the whole-rupee branch **is not a hunk** — it is four lines INSIDE the new `TryStatutoryRupees` with an ordering invariant coupling it to the branch above; splitting means hand-writing the method once without its fourth branch and rewriting a whole `<para>` of its XML doc, then putting both back; **both test files are NEW/untracked** and the whole-rupee cases share one fixture and one class-level doc with the guard and rollback cases, so commit (i) would have needed a **physically truncated new file whose own documentation described defects it did not yet fix**; **neither synthesized intermediate state would ever have been built or run** and the gate measures the combined state only; and the proposed line **does not partition the diff** anyway, because the decimal-comma 100× defect is a THIRD behaviour change belonging to neither half. **⇒ WHEN A REQUESTED SPLIT IS REFUSED, THE REFUSAL AND ITS REASONS GO IN THE COMMIT BODY. A commit that does not build is worse than a commit that does two things — but the reader is owed the argument, not the outcome.**
- **▶ FIVE CITATIONS RE-DERIVED WHILE WRITING THIS ENTRY AND PUSHED INTO `plan.md` AS CORRECTIONS, PLUS A SIXTH DESCRIPTION TIGHTENED — CORRECTED THERE AND HERE RATHER THAN COPIED FORWARD.** (⚠️ **This bullet was first written as "THREE CITATIONS" and enumerated only three of the five; the count and the enumeration are both repaired 2026-08-14. Every `plan.md` line number below was RE-DERIVED against the working tree after those corrections landed — they had ALL drifted, `plan.md` having grown from the 2,502 lines it carried at `fa651ae` to 2,553.**) **(i) 🔴 THE METHOD COUNT IS TWELVE, NOT ELEVEN.** The commit subject line and the commit body say **"eleven methods"**, and `plan.md` said it at three sites — **now corrected, reading "twelve" at `plan.md:2092`, `:2230` and `:2233`** — but every one of them then **enumerates twelve**, because the body's toggle-handler subtotal says "five toggle handlers" and lists **six**. Re-counted directly from the diff (`git show fa651ae -- src/Apex.Desktop/ViewModels/GstConfigViewModel.cs`, counting the added capture declarations): **six `Apply*` methods + six `On*Changed` toggle handlers = TWELVE**, each with a capture and a restore-before-revert. **The shipped code is right; the arithmetic in the prose is off by one and it is off by one in three places. Believe the enumeration, not the numeral.** **(ii) `Paisa.FromMoney` IS AT `src/Apex.Persistence.Sqlite/Paisa.cs:15`, NOT `:14`** — `:14` is its doc comment. `plan.md`'s W0-12 carry-forward (a) cited `:14` and **now reads `:15` (`plan.md:2205`, which records the correction rather than making it silently)**. **(iii) ⚠️ THE COMMIT BODY'S PROSE STATES THE GUARD'S BRANCH ORDER WRONG — DO NOT "RESTORE" IT FROM THE BODY.** The body reads "magnitude ceiling … → paisa-exactness … → sign/parse → whole rupee"; the **shipped source** is parse/sign (`:1411`) → magnitude (`:1423`) → paisa-exactness (`:1430`) → whole rupee (`:1444`), which is also what **`plan.md:2094-2107`** says — that block now states the correction outright, including that `fa651ae`'s body has it wrong and that, the commit being pushed and unamendable, `plan.md` is the only place the correction can live — and is the only order that works (you cannot compare a magnitude you have not parsed). **The code is right, the body's sentence is not — and the body itself argues that "the ordering is load-bearing", so a reader who trusts the prose over the file would break exactly the invariant the slice was written to establish.** **(iv) 🔴 `AcceptStatutoryConfig` RUNS NINE APPLY METHODS, NOT SEVEN — the same off-by-miscount class as (i), in a different row.** Re-counted at the source: `AcceptStatutoryConfig` (`GstConfigViewModel.cs:1788`) calls `Apply` (`:1790`), `ApplyTds` (`:1792`), `ApplyTcs` (`:1794`), `ApplyPf` (`:1798`), `ApplyEsi` (`:1801`), `ApplyPt` (`:1804`), `ApplySalaryTds` (`:1807`), `ApplyGratuity` (`:1810`) and `ApplyBonus` (`:1813`) — **NINE**, over `:1790-1813`. `plan.md` said "SEVEN" over a list of nine and now says NINE (`plan.md:2164-2170`). **(v) 🔴 THE `TrySave` INHERITANCE CLAIM WAS FALSE — the largest of the five, and it is refuted in full in carry-forward (b) below rather than restated here.** `plan.md` **and this entry** said `Apply` / `ApplyTds` / `ApplyTcs` "now inherit the widened `IsReportableSaveFailure` set through `TrySave`, so a `DbException` there is a message instead of a crash". Measured: `TrySave` has **seven** call sites, `Apply` (GST) reaches it **nowhere**, `ApplyTds` / `ApplyTcs` only on their DISABLE branch, and **five narrow filters survive** — so those paths still crash. Corrected at `plan.md:2245-2253` and in carry-forward (b) here. **(vi) ▶ AND A SIXTH — NOT A CITATION BUT AN OVERSTATEMENT, TIGHTENED.** The residue note said `JobWorkService.SetEnabled` (`src/Apex.Ledger/Services/JobWorkService.cs:44-58`) stamps `IsActive` / `UseForJobWork` / `AllowConsumption` on **"every Job-Work voucher type"**. Only the first of the three is universal: it sets the company flag (`:46`), then sets `IsActive` on **every** Job-Work base type (`:50-51`), `UseForJobWork` on **only the two Material types** (`:53-54`) and `AllowConsumption` on **only Material In** (`:55-56`). **The conclusion is unchanged — restoring one bool undoes none of it, so it needs a per-type capture and stays whole rather than half-fixed — but it now rests on what the method actually does.**
- **▶ CARRY-FORWARD (a) — 🔴 SEVEN UNGUARDED `Paisa.FromMoney` PATHS REMAIN OPEN. A DIFFERENT BUG CLASS, DELIBERATELY OUT OF THIS SLICE'S SCOPE.** W0-11's carry-forward (a) scoped the class to `Paisa.FromDecimal` and the three statutory fields; **the same throw is reachable through `Paisa.FromMoney` (`src/Apex.Persistence.Sqlite/Paisa.cs:15`, the same `ToPaisaExact`)** wherever a domain constructor takes a `Money` the UI parsed with a bare `decimal.TryParse`. **Re-verified at `fa651ae`: all seven owning view-models contain ZERO `IsPaisaExact` calls — none of them is guarded.** They are `BudgetMasterViewModel.cs`, `BillAllocationRowViewModel.cs`, `CostAllocationRowViewModel.cs`, `VoucherLineViewModel.cs`, `PosBillingViewModel.cs`, `SalaryStructureMasterViewModel.cs`, `PayHeadMasterViewModel.cs` — the per-site UI-parse ⇒ domain-construct ⇒ persist traces are enumerated in `plan.md:2202-2229` (re-derived 2026-08-14; this cited `:2162-2188`) and are not restated here. **🔴 THE SEVERITY CORRECTION THE REVIEW MADE, AND IT MATTERS: `BudgetMasterViewModel.cs:198` is the LOUDEST instance — `_company.AddBudget(budget);` at `:197` then `_storage.Save(_company);` at `:198` with NO `try`/`catch` at all, so a sub-paisa budget line is an UNHANDLED `InvalidOperationException` in the UI, i.e. a crash — but it is NOT the most dangerous.** A **silent wrong-figure divergence like `ApplyPt`'s** (a live Professional-Tax slab selected off a state the book does not have) is **worse per rupee and far easier to miss**. **⇒ SEQUENCE #1 FIRST BECAUSE IT IS CHEAP AND VISIBLE, NOT BECAUSE IT IS THE WORST. A loud crash and a silent divergence are different severities and the record must not collapse them.**

  2. **⚠️ `CompanyFactory` DEFAULTS THE FINANCIAL-YEAR START TO THE *CALENDAR* YEAR, SO A COMPANY CREATED IN JAN–MAR GETS A FUTURE-DATED FY START.** `src/Apex.Ledger/Services/CompanyFactory.cs:22` is `var fyStart = financialYearStart ?? new DateOnly(DateTime.Today.Year, 4, 1);` and `booksBeginFrom` defaults to it (`:23`). Create a company on 10-Feb-2026 with no explicit date and it opens with an FY starting **1-Apr-2026** — **after the day it was created** — so every voucher back-dated into the real running FY (Apr-2025 → Mar-2026) falls outside the book. **This is directly in W0-2's path** (that item's stated purpose includes **unblocking prior-FY books**, and creation today captures one field: Name). **⇒ THE FY DEFAULT MUST BE THE FY CONTAINING TODAY (1-Apr of `Today.Year` when the month is ≥ April, else 1-Apr of `Today.Year − 1`) — fix it as part of W0-2, and pin it with a Jan–Mar fixture date, not `DateTime.Today`.**

(written AFTER, retroactively, because nothing authorised it). `plan.md` was **2,954 lines** at `7ae0894` (⚠️ CORRECTED 2026-08-15 from "2,943"; `git show 7ae0894:plan.md | wc -l`), and is **2,957** after the correction above. **⚠️ THE COMMIT BOUNDARY IS TWO COMMITS, NOT ONE:** W0-13's source+tests belong to its row and the six docs + the grounding file to W0-14's.

The capture cost six lines; the deferral would have shipped the very shape the row was written to undo.**

the `IsStorable` figure holds and the `ErrorFor` figure does not.** The 13 are `BillAllocationRowViewModel.cs:81` · `BudgetMasterViewModel.cs:142` · `CostAllocationRowViewModel.cs:86` · `PayHeadMasterViewModel.cs:335`,`:349`,`:380`,`:446` · `PosBillingViewModel.cs:91`,`:100`,`:630`,`:669` · `SalaryStructureMasterViewModel.cs:307` · `VoucherLineViewModel.cs:640`. **Flagged, not resolved — believe the enumeration, not the numeral** (this project's standing rule, earned on W0-12's "eleven methods over a list of twelve").

(⚠️ **UNVERIFIED in this run — the suite could not be re-run; these two figures come from the slice's own transcript, not from a measurement made while writing this entry.**) Its replacement was briefed to **establish each finding's state BEFORE acting**, and found the dead agent had closed nearly every SOURCE finding while leaving most of the **PINNING** undone — so re-applying the brief blindly would have DOUBLED the edits. **⇒ AFTER AN AGENT DEATH, THE FIRST TASK OF THE REPLACEMENT IS TO MEASURE THE TREE, NOT TO EXECUTE THE BRIEF. This is the fifth death in this project to leave files on disk (see the 2026-08-04 entry's lesson 3, four in one session); the standing rule "liveness lies, and so does the working tree" now has a corollary: a silent death is INDISTINGUISHABLE from a no-op except by measurement.**

The lever survives in the shipped tests and is verifiable: `tests/Apex.Desktop.Tests/VoucherEntrySubPaisaFrontLineGuardTests.cs:125-127`, `StatutoryConfigGstTdsTcsRollbackTests.cs:131`,`:144-146` and `PayrollBudgetSubPaisaAndRollbackTests.cs:106-108`, each carrying the comment `// same primary key twice`. **It is also the ONLY lever that discriminates the widened list from the shipped narrow filter** — a sub-paisa lever cannot, because the narrow filter already matched `InvalidOperationException`. **⇒ A DESIGN CLAIM THAT A PATH "ALREADY UNWINDS" IS A HYPOTHESIS UNTIL A CONSTRUCTED FAILURE SHOWS THE AGGREGATE AFTERWARDS. Build the failure; do not reason about the catch.**
